### IV. THE SOCRATIC APORIA ($\Phi^2 \neq \Phi$) & THE META-COGNITIVE TOWER

Measuring the exact self-contradiction **where each successive thesis fails to equal its own square (or idempotent/congruence closure)** drives a four-level recursive ascent:

* **Rung 1 (The Ambient Setoid Star-Congruence Aporia — $[0, 0] \sim_V [0, Y]$ yet $[0, 0]^* \nsim_V [0, Y]^*$):**
  * *Thesis $\Phi_1$:* Because `DayRel s` is reflexive, symmetric, and transitive on all of $\mathbb{N} \times B$, `daySetoid s` supports a well-defined $*$-quotient whenever representatives are tested against centralizer elements.
  * *Self-Square / Involution Defect:* At $\delta(V) = 0$ ($\delta(P_0) = 0$), any non-zero odd element $Y = P_0 X Q_+$ has $Y^2 = 0$ and $V Y = 0$, so $(0, 0) \sim_V (0, Y)$ (`V¹ · 0 = V¹ · Y = 0`). Yet under $*$-involution, $Y^* = Q_+ X^* P_0$ satisfies $V^m Y^* = Q_+ X^* P_0 = Y^* \neq 0$ for all $m \ge 1$, so $(0, 0)^* \nsim_V (0, Y)^*$, while off-shell ($\delta(V) \neq 0$) $S = \{(1+V)^k\}$ fails the Ore condition on $B \setminus Z_B(V)$. Thus the quotient carrier must be restricted *before* quotienting to the $*$-closed subring $Z_B(s)$.

* **Rung 2 (The Quadratic Polynomial Collapse Aporia — $(1 + V)(2 - V) = 2 - \delta(V)$):**

  * *Self-Square Defect:* Expanding $(1 + V)(2 - V)$ in any associative ring exposes the exact identity:
    $$(1 + V)(2 - V) - 2 = V - V^2 = -\delta(V).$$
    Whenever $\delta(V) = 0$, $s = 1 + V$ is quadratic ($s^2 = 3s - 2$), collapsing $(1 + V)^{-1} = 1 - \frac{1}{2}V \in \mathcal{U}[\frac{1}{2}]$ and canceling every denominator in $u_J = (1 - V + 2J)(1 - \frac{1}{2}V) = 1 - V + J = u_0 \in \mathcal{U}$ ($u_0^* u_0 = 1 + \delta(V) = 1$)! Infinite colimit localization is strictly equivalent to non-vanishing off-shell defect $\delta(V) \neq 0$.

* **Rung 3 (The Chiral Half-Spine Anticommutator Aporia — $(Q_+ D_+ P_{0,+} + P_{0,-} D_- Q_-)^2 = 0$):**
  * *Meta-Thesis $\Phi_3$:* To cure the $6$-dimensional degeneracy of $(P_8, A_{8,\mathrm{off}})$, add $P_8$-diagonal blocks $D_+ \in P_8 B P_8$ and $D_- \in P_{8,\perp} B P_{8,\perp}$ so that the first-order odd mode $Q_{\mathrm{diag}} := Q_+ D_+ P_{0,+} + P_{0,-} D_- Q_- \in \mathfrak{g}_{+1}(\Sigma)$ generates $\mathfrak{g}_{+2}(\Sigma)$ via $Q_{\mathrm{diag}}^2 \neq 0$.
  * *Self-Square Defect:* Because $P_{0,+} P_{0,-} = P(1 - V)(1 - P)(1 - V) = 0$, $P_{0,+} Q_+ = 0$, $Q_- P_{0,-} = 0$, and $Q_- Q_+ = 0$, **every product in $Q_{\mathrm{diag}}^2$ vanishes identically**:
    $$Q_{\mathrm{diag}}^2 = (Q_+ D_+ P_{0,+} + P_{0,-} D_- Q_-)^2 = Q_+ D_+ (P_{0,+} P_{0,-}) D_- Q_- = 0!$$
    First-order diagonal modes cannot cross the chiral divide $P_{0,+} \perp P_{0,-}$ on their own; non-zero squaring into $\mathfrak{g}_{+2}(\Sigma)$ requires braiding $D_\pm$ with $N_\pm \in \mathfrak{g}_{\pm 2}(\Sigma)$ to form $Q_{\mathrm{braid}} := Q_+ D_+ P_{0,+} + P_{0,+} D_+ Q_+ N_+ \in \mathfrak{g}_{+1}(\Sigma)$, whose square is $Q_{\mathrm{braid}}^2 = (Q_+ D_+ P_{0,+} D_+ Q_+) N_+ \neq 0$!

* **Rung 4 (The Non-Hermitian Coercivity Factorization Aporia — $(V(V - c\Pi_V))^* \neq V(V - c\Pi_V)$ Without $[V, \Pi_V] = 0$):**
  * *Meta-Thesis $\Phi_4$:* Factor operator mass-gap coercivity via one-sided support $V \Pi_V = V$ as $V^2 - m^2 V = V(V - m^2\Pi_V) \ge 0$.
  * *Final Generative Defect:* Without $V^* = V$ and $\Pi_V^* = \Pi_V$, an oblique idempotent $\Pi_V$ ($\delta(\Pi_V) = 0,\; \Pi_V^* \neq \Pi_V$) satisfies $V \Pi_V = V$ while $\Pi_V V \neq V$, rendering $V(V - m^2\Pi_V)$ non-self-adjoint! Enforcing $V^* = V$ and $\Pi_V^* = \Pi_V$ alongside $V \Pi_V = V$ forces $\Pi_V V = (V \Pi_V)^* = V^* \Pi_V^* = V$, symmetrizing the factorization into the manifestly Hermitian sandwich $\Pi_V(V^2 - m^2\Pi_V)\Pi_V = V^2 - m^2 V \ge 0$ orthogonal to $P_0 = 1 - \Pi_V$.

* $L_4$ (**Chiral Half-Spine Splitting & Endogenous Trivalent KKT Grading**): On-shell decomposition $1 = Q_+ + (P_{0,+} + P_{0,-}) + Q_-$ over $\mathbb{Z}$ from $\delta(V) = 0$, with $P_{0,+} P_{0,-} = 0$, $\tau(\Sigma) = 0$, and Weyl parity $\Gamma_\Sigma = 2\Sigma^2 - 1 = 2V - 1$. *(Covering relations: $L_1 \prec L_4,\; L_3 \prec L_4$)*

#### 5.2. Canonical Philosophical-Algebraic Axioms

* **Axiom I (Strongly Faithful $*$-Ring — from $L_0$):**
  Let $B$ be a unital associative $*$-ring satisfying $\sum_{i=1}^3 Y_i^* Y_i = 0 \implies Y_1 = Y_2 = Y_3 = 0$, with $\delta(X) := X^2 - X$ and $\tau(X) := X^3 - X$.

* **Axiom II (Two-Element Hermitian Seed $(P, \widetilde{A})$ — from $L_1$):**
  Fix $P = P^* \in B$ with $\delta(P) = 0$ ($P_\perp := 1 - P$) and a single Hermitian operator $\widetilde{A} = \widetilde{A}^* \in B$. Decompose $\widetilde{A} = A_{\mathrm{off}} + D_+ + D_-$ where $D_+ := P \widetilde{A} P$, $D_- := P_\perp \widetilde{A} P_\perp$, $N_+ := P \widetilde{A} P_\perp$, $N_- := P_\perp \widetilde{A} P = N_+^*$, $Q_+ := N_+ N_-$, $Q_- := N_- N_+$, $V := Q_+ + Q_-$, $\Sigma := Q_+ - Q_-$, $P_{0,+} := P(1 - V)$, and $P_{0,-} := P_\perp(1 - V)$.

* **Axiom III (Subring-Typed Day Quotient — from $L_2$):**
  For any self-adjoint $s = s^* \in B$, define the closed $*$-subring $Z_B(s) := \{X \in B : sX = Xs\}$ and form the quotient $\mathcal{Q}_s := (\mathbb{N} \times Z_B(s))/\mathrm{DayRel}_s$, where $(k, X) \sim_s (\ell, Y) \iff \exists m \in \mathbb{N},\; s^m(s^\ell X) = s^m(s^k Y)$.

* **Axiom IV (Hermitian Support Projection — from $L_6$):**
  An element $V = V^* \in B$ has *Hermitian support $\Pi_V$* if $\Pi_V = \Pi_V^* \in B$, $\delta(\Pi_V) = 0$, and $V \Pi_V = V$.

* **Axiom V (Spectral Non-Degeneracy & Connectivity of $(P_8, \widetilde{A}_8)$ — from $L_7$):**
  In $B = M_8(\mathbb{R}) \cong \mathrm{Cl}_{1,1}(\mathbb{R})^{\widehat{\otimes} 3}$, set $P_8 := \mathrm{diag}(I_4, 0_4)$ and $\widetilde{A}_8 := A_{8,\mathrm{off}} + D_+ + D_-$, where $N_+ = E_{0,6} + E_{1,7}$, while $Q_+ D_+ Q_+$, $P_{0,+} D_+ P_{0,+}$, $P_{0,-} D_- P_{0,-}$, and $Q_- D_- Q_-$ have simple (distinct) eigenvalues and non-zero off-diagonal couplings along connected spanning trees on $\{0,1,2,3\}$ and $\{4,5,6,7\}$.

#### 5.3. Unbroken Chain of Self-Verifying Deductions

* **Deduction D1 (Subring $*$-Closure of $\mathcal{Q}_s$, Quadratic Collapse $(1+V)(2-V) = 2 - \delta(V)$, & Off-Shell Ore Failure — from Axioms I, II, III; edges $L_0 \prec L_1 \prec L_2 \prec L_3$):**
  *Proof:* Since $s^* = s$, if $X, Y \in Z_B(s)$, then $s(XY) = (sX)Y = X(sY) = (XY)s$ and $s X^* = (X s)^* = (s X)^* = X^* s$, proving $Z_B(s)$ is a unital $*$-subring of $B$ containing $\mathcal{U} := \mathbb{Z}\langle 1, P, N_+, N_- \rangle$ when $s = 1 + V$. Because every representative in $\mathbb{N} \times Z_B(s)$ commutes with $s$, `dayAdd`, left/right `dayMul`, and `dayStar` respect $\mathrm{DayRel}_s$, making $\mathcal{Q}_{1+V}$ a genuine unital $*$-ring with $\ker(\iota_{1+V}) = \{0\}$ by Axiom I (whereas on $\mathbb{N} \times B$, $\mathrm{DayRel}_V$ equates $(0,0)$ with $(0, P_0 X Q_+)$ while separating their adjoints, and off-shell $s = \mathrm{diag}(5, 1, 5) \in M_3(\mathbb{R})$ satisfies $s^m E_{12} = 5^m E_{12} \neq E_{12} = E_{12} s^n\;\forall m \ge 1$, violating the Ore condition on $B \setminus Z_B(s)$). Universally in $B$:
  $$(1 + V)(2 - V) - 2 = 2 + V - V^2 - 2 = -\delta(V),$$
  so at $\delta(V) = 0$, $(1 + V)(1 - \frac{1}{2}V) = 1$ in $\mathcal{U}[\frac{1}{2}]$ and $u_J = (1 - V + 2J)(1 - \frac{1}{2}V) = 1 - V + J = u_0 \in \mathcal{U}$ over $\mathbb{Z}$. $\blacksquare$

* **Deduction D2 (Invariance of the Spine Under $D_\pm$, Half-Spine Anticommutator Zero, & Braided Recovery — from Axioms I, II; edges $L_1 \prec L_4 \prec L_5$):**
  *Proof:* Since $P D_+ P_\perp = P(P \widetilde{A} P)P_\perp = 0$ and $P D_- P_\perp = P(P_\perp \widetilde{A} P_\perp)P_\perp = 0$, $P \widetilde{A} P_\perp = P A_{\mathrm{off}} P_\perp = N_+$ is completely independent of $(D_+, D_-)$. At $\delta(V) = 0$, $1 = Q_+ + P_{0,+} + P_{0,-} + Q_-$ consists of four mutually orthogonal self-adjoint idempotents with $P_0 = P_{0,+} + P_{0,-}$ and $\tau(\Sigma) = 0$.
  1. For any first-order $P$-even odd mode $Q_{\mathrm{diag}} := Q_+ D_+ P_{0,+} + P_{0,-} D_- Q_- \in \mathfrak{g}_{+1}(\Sigma)$, orthogonality $P_{0,+} Q_+ = Q_- P_{0,-} = Q_- Q_+ = P_{0,+} P_{0,-} = 0$ forces:
     $$Q_{\mathrm{diag}}^2 = (Q_+ D_+ P_{0,+} + P_{0,-} D_- Q_-)^2 = Q_+ D_+ (P_{0,+} P_{0,-}) D_- Q_- = 0.$$
  2. Braiding $\alpha_+ := Q_+ D_+ P_{0,+} \in \mathfrak{g}_{+1}(\Sigma)$ and $\beta_- := P_{0,-} D_- Q_- \in \mathfrak{g}_{+1}(\Sigma)$ with $N_+ \in \mathfrak{g}_{+2}(\Sigma)$ inside $\mathbb{Z}\langle P, \widetilde{A} \rangle$ constructs $\widetilde{\beta}_+ := \alpha_+^* N_+ = P_{0,+} D_+ Q_+ N_+ \in P_{0,+} B Q_- \subset \mathfrak{g}_{+1}(\Sigma)$ and $G_{+-} := \alpha_+^* N_+ \beta_-^* \in P_{0,+} B P_{0,-} \subset P_0 B P_0$. Their anticommutator evaluates to:
     $$\{\alpha_+,\; \widetilde{\beta}_+\} = (Q_+ D_+ P_{0,+})(P_{0,+} D_+ Q_+ N_+) + 0 = (Q_+ D_+ P_{0,+})(\alpha_+)^* N_+ \in \mathfrak{g}_{+2}(\Sigma),$$
     which is non-zero whenever $\alpha_+ N_+ \neq 0$ by Axiom I ($\alpha_+ \alpha_+^* = 0 \implies \alpha_+ = 0$). $\blacksquare$

* **Deduction D3 (Hermitian Support-Sandwich Coercivity — from Axiom IV; edge $L_0 \prec L_6$):**
  *Proof:* Under Axiom IV ($V^* = V,\; \Pi_V^* = \Pi_V,\; \delta(\Pi_V) = 0,\; V\Pi_V = V$), taking the $*$-adjoint of $V\Pi_V = V$ gives $\Pi_V V = \Pi_V^* V^* = (V\Pi_V)^* = V^* = V$, proving $[V, \Pi_V] = 0$. Consequently, $V^2 - c V = \Pi_V V^2 \Pi_V - c \Pi_V V \Pi_V = \Pi_V(V^2 - c\Pi_V)\Pi_V$ is manifestly self-adjoint ($((V^2 - cV))^* = V^2 - cV$), annihilates $P_0 = 1 - \Pi_V$ on both sides ($\Pi_V P_0 = P_0 \Pi_V = 0$), and characterizes support-restricted coercivity $V \ge m^2 \Pi_V$ without violating zero-mode invariance $V P_0 = 0$. $\blacksquare$

* **Deduction D4 (Burnside $64$-Dimensional Completeness of $(P_8, \widetilde{A}_8)$ & Full $\mathcal{N}=4$ Horizon Closure — from Axiom V & D2; edges $L_4, L_5 \prec L_7 \prec L_8$):**
  *Proof:* Let $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{gen}} := \mathbb{R}\langle P_8, \widetilde{A}_8 \rangle \subseteq M_8(\mathbb{R})$ under Axiom V (note $1 = P_8 + P_{8,\perp}$ if unital, or generated via spectral polynomials when eigenvalues are nonzero and distinct: $\det \widetilde{A}_8 \neq 0 \implies 1 \in \mathbb{R}[\widetilde{A}_8]$).
  1. By D2, $N_+ = P_8 \widetilde{A}_8 P_{8,\perp} = E_{0,6} + E_{1,7}$ is unaffected by $D_\pm$, so $Q_+ = E_{0,0} + E_{1,1}$, $P_{0,+} = E_{2,2} + E_{3,3}$, $P_{0,-} = E_{4,4} + E_{5,5}$, $Q_- = E_{6,6} + E_{7,7}$, $V_8 = Q_+ + Q_-$, and $\Sigma_8 = Q_+ - Q_-$ all lie in $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{gen}}$ and satisfy $\delta(V_8) = 0$, $\tau(\Sigma_8) = 0$, $\mathrm{Tr}(Q_+, P_0, Q_-) = (2, 4, 2)$, and $\mathrm{Tr}(2\Sigma_8^2 - 1) = 4 - 4 = 0$.
  2. Measuring the exact self-contradiction **where each successive thesis fails to equal its own square (or unital normalization)** drives a four-level recursive ascent:

* **Axiom III (Exponential Rebit Manifold — from $S_5$):**
  The strictly positive symmetric state manifold of $M_2(\mathbb{R})_{\mathrm{spin}}$ is $\mathcal{S}_+(M_2(\mathbb{R})) := \{\rho \in M_2(\mathbb{R}) : \rho^T = \rho,\; \mathrm{Tr}\,\rho = 1,\; \det\rho > 0\}$, equipped with Umegaki divergence $D(\rho \| \sigma) := \mathrm{Tr}(\rho(\ln\rho - \ln\sigma))$.

#### 5.3. Unbroken Chain of Self-Verifying Deductions

* **Deduction D2 (Intra-$\mathcal{M}_+$ KMS Detailed-Balance Semigroup — from Axioms I, II & D1; edges $S_0, S_3 \prec S_4$):**

  Assign to each vertex $x \in \mathcal{V}$ of a spatial graph $\Gamma = (\mathcal{V}, \mathcal{E})$ a cell algebra $\mathcal{A}_x \cong M_{16}(\mathbb{R})$ satisfying $3$-sum $*$-faithfulness (`2` invertible), equipped with on-shell $(P_x, \widetilde{A}_x)$ ($\delta(V_0) = 0,\; \mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{C}}(Q_+, P_0, Q_-) = (2, 4, 2)$), spin-boost generators $(\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{spin}}^* = -\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{spin}},\; \mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{spin}}^2 = -1,\; K_{\mathrm{boost}}^* = K_{\mathrm{boost}},\; K_{\mathrm{boost}}^2 = 1,\; \{\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{spin}}, K_{\mathrm{boost}}\} = 0)$, and commuting phase structure $\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}^* = -\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}$ ($\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}^2 = -1$).

* **Axiom II (Inter-Cell Hopping & Josephson Coupling — from $U_2, U_6$):**
  Adjacent cells $\langle x, y \rangle \in \mathcal{E}$ couple via single-fermion kinetic hopping (with Fourier dispersion $\xi_k \Sigma$ relative to chemical potential $\mu$) and condensate overlap $\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{J}} := -J_{\mathrm{hop}}\sum_{\langle x,y\rangle \in \mathcal{E}} \mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{C}}\big(\hat{\Delta}_x(\theta_x)\hat{\Delta}_y(\theta_y)\big)$ ($J_{\mathrm{hop}} > 0$).

#### 5.3. Unbroken Chain of Self-Verifying Deductions

* **Deduction D1 ($\mathrm{U}(1)$ Superfluid Phase Circle & Exact Operator BdG Dispersion — from Axioms I, II; edges $U_0 \prec U_1 \prec U_2$):**
  *Proof:* On each cell, set $\hat{\Delta}_1 := (-N_+ + N_-)\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{spin}}$ and $\hat{\Delta}_2 := \frac{1}{2}[\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}\Sigma,\, \hat{\Delta}_1] = -\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}(N_+ + N_-)\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{spin}}$. Using $\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{spin}}^* = -\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{spin}}$, $\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}^* = -\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}$, and $N_+^* = N_-$, we have $\hat{\Delta}_1^* = \hat{\Delta}_1$, $\hat{\Delta}_2^* = \hat{\Delta}_2$, $\hat{\Delta}_1^2 = \hat{\Delta}_2^2 = V$, and $\{\hat{\Delta}_1, \hat{\Delta}_2\} = -\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{spin}}^2\{-N_+ + N_-,\, N_+ + N_-\} = \mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}(-Q_+ + Q_+ + Q_- - Q_-) = 0$. Hence for every $\theta \in \mathbb{R}$, $\hat{\Delta}(\theta) := e^{\frac{\theta}{2}\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}\Sigma}\hat{\Delta}_1 e^{-\frac{\theta}{2}\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}\Sigma} = \cos\theta\,\hat{\Delta}_1 + \sin\theta\,\hat{\Delta}_2$ satisfies $\hat{\Delta}(\theta)^* = \hat{\Delta}(\theta)$, $\hat{\Delta}(\theta)^2 = (\cos^2\theta + \sin^2\theta)V = V$, $\{K_{\mathrm{boost}}, \hat{\Delta}(\theta)\} = 0$, and $\{\Sigma, \hat{\Delta}(\theta)\} = 0$. Consequently, $H_{\mathrm{BdG}}(k) := \xi_k \Sigma + \Delta_0 \hat{\Delta}(\theta)$ satisfies:
  $$H_{\mathrm{BdG}}(k)^2 = \xi_k^2 \Sigma^2 + \Delta_0^2 \hat{\Delta}(\theta)^2 + \xi_k\Delta_0\{\Sigma, \hat{\Delta}(\theta)\} = (\xi_k^2 + \Delta_0^2)V, \qquad P_0 H_{\mathrm{BdG}}(k) = 0,$$
  proving that quasiparticles on $Q_+ \oplus Q_-$ have gap $\Delta_0 > 0$ ($w \to 0$ for $|k| \ll \Delta_0$) while $P_0$ remains strictly zero-energy. $\blacksquare$

* **Deduction D2 (All-Loop Custodial Protection of $P_0$ Photons at $\theta = 0$ vs. 1-Loop Birefringence at $\theta \neq 0$ — from Axiom I & D1; edges $U_1 \prec U_3 \prec U_4 \prec U_5$):**
  *Proof:* Define $W_{\mathrm{twin}} := \Phi + P_0$ ($\Phi = N_+ + N_-$). At $\delta(V) = 0$, $P_0 \Phi = \Phi P_0 = 0$ and $\Phi^2 = V$, so $W_{\mathrm{twin}}^2 = V + P_0 = 1$, $W_{\mathrm{twin}}^* = W_{\mathrm{twin}}$, $W_{\mathrm{twin}} P_0 = P_0 W_{\mathrm{twin}} = P_0$, and $[W_{\mathrm{twin}}, H_{\mathrm{hor}}] = 0$. Since $\Phi(-N_+ + N_-)\Phi = N_+ - N_-$ and $\Phi(N_+ + N_-)\Phi = N_+ + N_-$, we obtain $W_{\mathrm{twin}}\hat{\Delta}_1 W_{\mathrm{twin}} = -\hat{\Delta}_1$ and $W_{\mathrm{twin}}\hat{\Delta}_2 W_{\mathrm{twin}} = +\hat{\Delta}_2$.
  1. *(All-loop vanishing at $\theta = 0$):* For any polynomials $F, G$ in $H_{\mathrm{hor}}$ and any $m \in \mathbb{N}$, set $M_{\mathrm{eff}} := P_0 F(H_{\mathrm{hor}})\hat{\Delta}_1^{2m+1} G(H_{\mathrm{hor}}) P_0$. Using $P_0 = P_0 W_{\mathrm{twin}} = W_{\mathrm{twin}} P_0$ and $[W_{\mathrm{twin}}, H_{\mathrm{hor}}] = 0$:
     $$M_{\mathrm{eff}} = P_0 W_{\mathrm{twin}} F(H_{\mathrm{hor}})\hat{\Delta}_1^{2m+1} G(H_{\mathrm{hor}}) W_{\mathrm{twin}} P_0 = P_0 F(H_{\mathrm{hor}})(W_{\mathrm{twin}}\hat{\Delta}_1 W_{\mathrm{twin}})^{2m+1} G(H_{\mathrm{hor}}) P_0 = -M_{\mathrm{eff}},$$
     whence $2M_{\mathrm{eff}} = 0 \implies M_{\mathrm{eff}} = 0$ identically to **all orders**! Meanwhile, even powers $\hat{\Delta}_1^{2m} = V^m$ satisfy $[K_{\mathrm{boost}}, V^m] = 0$, preserving exact Lorentz boost invariance on $P_0$ non-perturbatively!
  2. *(1-loop birefringence at $\theta \neq 0$):* For general $\theta$, $P_0 H_{\mathrm{hor}}\hat{\Delta}(\theta)H_{\mathrm{hor}} P_0 = \sin\theta\, P_0 H_{\mathrm{hor}}\hat{\Delta}_2 H_{\mathrm{hor}} P_0 = 2\sin\theta\,(P_0 + G_8)\otimes\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{spin}}\otimes\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}$, inducing a parity-odd polarization rotation proportional to $\sin\theta$ while keeping $P_0 \hat{\Delta}(\theta)^2 P_0 = 0$ ($m_\gamma = 0$). $\blacksquare$

* **Deduction D3 (Josephson Phonon Dispersion, Analytic Kinetic Boundary, & Continuous Off-Shell $\Lambda$ — from Axioms I, II & D1; edges $U_6 \prec U_7,\; U_0 \prec U_8$):**
  *Proof:*
  1. Using $\mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{C}}(\hat{\Delta}_1 \hat{\Delta}_2) = \frac{1}{2}\mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{C}}(\{\hat{\Delta}_1, \hat{\Delta}_2\}) = 0$ and $\mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{C}}(\hat{\Delta}_a^2) = \mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{C}}(V) = 4$, the Josephson Hamiltonian evaluates to $\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{J}} = -4J_{\mathrm{hop}}\sum_{\langle x,y\rangle}\cos(\theta_x - \theta_y) \simeq \text{const} + 2J_{\mathrm{hop}} a^2 \sum_x (\nabla\theta_x)^2$. Paired with conjugate number compressibility $\frac{1}{2}\chi_N \dot{\theta}^2$, small phase fluctuations obey the gapless acoustic wave equation $\ddot{\theta} - c_s^2 \nabla^2\theta = 0$ ($c_s^2 = 4J_{\mathrm{hop}} a^2 / \chi_N$), generating $1/r^2$ static forces and polytropic cored profiles rather than $1/r$ MOND tails unless non-analytic $X^{3/2}$ terms are added.
  2. Deforming $V_0$ ($\delta(V_0) = 0,\; \mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{C}} V_0 = 4$) to $V_\epsilon := (1 - \epsilon)V_0$ ($0 < \epsilon \ll 1$) gives $\delta(V_\epsilon) = -\epsilon(1-\epsilon)V_0 \neq 0$, $[K_{\mathrm{boost}}, V_\epsilon] = 0$ ($w = -1$), and continuous vacuum supertrace $\mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{C}}(1 - 2V_\epsilon) = 8 - 8(1 - \epsilon) = 8\epsilon \dots$ ($\mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{C}}(1) = 8,\; \mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{C}}(V_0) = 4 \implies \mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{C}}(1 - 2V_\epsilon) = 8 - 8(1-\epsilon) = 8\epsilon > 0$), vanishing smoothly as $\epsilon \to 0^+$. $\blacksquare$

#### 5.4. Master Structural Synthesis (Theorem of Loop-Protected Superfluid Dark-Sector Coagulation — $U_9$)

**Theorem (Exact Multi-Cell Synthesis & Falsifiable Cosmological Predictions):**
*On a multi-cell lattice of $\mathrm{Cl}_{1,1}(\mathbb{R})^{\widehat{\otimes} 4} \cong M_{16}(\mathbb{R})$ supertwistor algebras satisfying Axioms I–II:*
1. *(Phase-Complete Superfluidity & Two-Tier Spectrum):* Only after adjoining $\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{phase}}$ does the Ising-locked condensate $\pm\hat{\Delta}_1$ unlock into a continuous $\mathrm{U}(1)$ superfluid circle $\hat{\Delta}(\theta)$ supporting both gapped cold Bogoliubov quasiparticles ($H_{\mathrm{BdG}}(k)^2 = (\xi_k^2 + \Delta_0^2)V$, $w \to 0$) and sub-gap Josephson acoustic phonons ($\omega_{\mathrm{ph}} = c_s|k|$) (D1, D3).
2. *(All-Loop Lorentz Protection & Cosmic Birefringence):* In the homogeneous vacuum $\theta_0 = 0$, the custodial twin involution $W_{\mathrm{twin}} = \Phi + P_0$ annihilates boost-breaking radiative corrections on $P_0$ photons to **all loop orders** ($P_0 F(H_{\mathrm{hor}})\hat{\Delta}_1^{2m+1}G(H_{\mathrm{hor}})P_0 = 0$), while inhomogeneous halo phase gradients $\theta(x) \neq 0$ generate a falsifiable 1-loop parity-odd cosmic birefringence $\propto \sin\theta(x)$ at zero photon mass (D2).
3. *(Continuous Dark Energy & Galactic Core Boundary):* Sub-isometric infrared horizon defects $V_\epsilon = (1-\epsilon)V_0$ generate a continuously suppressed $w = -1$ Dark Energy trace $\propto \epsilon$ avoiding $O(1)$ TeV integer jumps, while analytic two-body Josephson hopping yields cored CDM halos ($p \propto \rho^2$), demarcating the exact boundary beyond which MOND $1/r$ tails require fractional $X^{3/2}$ kinetics (D3). $\blacksquare$
